# Open Work

This is the consolidated index for unfinished verification, cleanup, feature work, and interactive confirmation. Detailed results remain in the linked plans and matrices. An automated pass never closes a manual host or visual item.

The 2026-07-28 capability groups, dependency-safe commit series, focused gates, and clean integration evidence are recorded in [Stabilization Inventory](stabilization-inventory.md).

## Capability Chain Status

| Area | Internal status | Remaining closing evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Dynamic matrix decompositions | Allocator-owned reusable LU, QR, and SVD provide solves, inverse, determinant, rank, condition number, pseudoinverse, and least squares. Allocation failure, aliasing, malformed retained state, teardown, cross-target compilation, installed consumers, and extreme finite scaling are covered. | No additional internal closing evidence. Product numerical workloads remain useful before an API freeze. |
| HRTF | Standard-file parsing, multi-distance interpolation, delay alignment, spectral interpolation, motion schedules, streaming publication, filter crossfades, room geometry, material responses, synthetic fixtures, two licensed public datasets, an independent full-response numerical comparison, and pinned libmysofa filter and convolution parity are implemented. | Motion-tracker and product dataset policy, comparison with an established complete room renderer, and headphone audition. |
| MP3 | Layer III decoding and PCM encoding cover tonality, temporal masking, joint stereo, CBR and VBR, reservoir packing, protected frames, gapless metadata, Xing, Info, VBRI, ID3, seeking, corruption recovery, an AndroidX VBRI input, and FFmpeg, LAME, Shine, and Helix encoder fixtures. | Additional implementation families and audition. |
| Spatial rendering | HOA radial adaptation, screen policy, dual-band decoding, layout generation, independent full-output HOA references, pinned libspatialaudio loudspeaker-render parity, ADM routing, Objects and Binaural timelines, HRTF rendering, and mathematical fixtures are implemented. | Comparison with an established complete room renderer, loudspeaker audition, and headphone audition. |
| Vorbis | Encoding, decoding, quality control, corruption handling, chaining, seeking, recovery, concealment policies, external encoder fixtures, independent decoder fixtures, complete FFmpeg, Xiph, and stb_vorbis PCM comparisons, and Xiph packet-loss concealment parity are implemented. | A fixed-point decoder family and audition. |
| LV2 | Patch properties, state, options, Worker, Programs, logging, dynamic-topology projection, toolkit-neutral and native UI paths, generated metadata, ABI fixtures, schema checks, and lifecycle stress are implemented. | External-host validation for reverse mapping, Worker, state, topology, and native UI ownership. |
| Linux accessibility | Native AT-SPI semantics, text, geometry, events, clipboard transports, lifecycle containment, sanitizer fixtures, and cross-target compilation are implemented. | Live Orca or Accerciser confirmation, live Wayland clipboard confirmation, and manual visual confirmation. |
| General hardening | Malformed-input sweeps, allocation checks, realtime source policies, public retained-state validation, documentation tracking, temporary-artifact containment, transactional bundle publication, and cache-budget enforcement are implemented. | External and manual rows above remain. Internal installed-package and complete ReleaseSafe reruns pass after cleanup. |

## Automated Verification History

This section contains chronological snapshots, not the live work queue. Each row records the status and evidence available on its date. Later gates may supersede old pending compiler, cache, tool, or environment claims. Use the current sections below, [Capability Matrix](capability-matrix.md), and [Roadmap](roadmap.md) for present gaps.

The established spatial-renderer gate pins libmysofa 1.3.5 and libspatialaudio 0.4.1 to immutable source revisions, archive hashes, and their BSD-3-Clause and LGPL-2.1-or-later licenses. Libmysofa matches eight complete unnormalized measured filters plus per-ear delays across the 44.1 kHz HUTUBS and 48 kHz Viking datasets, and each external filter passes a complete impulse render through the project convolver. Its 6,144 checked samples measure peak error `7.451e-8` and normalized RMS error `5.395e-8`. Libspatialaudio supplies independent preset matrices and complete 5.1 or 7.1 render buffers for first-, second-, and third-order three-dimensional HOA at 44.1, 48, and 96 kHz. The project reproduces 5,654 rendered samples across FuMa, N3D, SN3D, canonical, and permuted component orders with peak error `1.192e-7` and normalized RMS error `6.570e-8`. The comparison rejects silent, short, malformed, and non-finite references. Its deterministic source runner covers accepted installations, downloads, hashes, builds, TERM exit 143, status preservation, and partial-directory cleanup for both libraries. The required build step fails explicitly when either library prefix or either public SOFA fixture is missing. The focused ReleaseSafe HRTF and HOA gate passes 26/26 steps and 169/169 tests, including the native references and every supported cross-target compile. Installed-package consumers pass 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 420/420 steps and 7,384/7,386 tests with the two documented platform-service skips. GitHub Actions run `31462945649` passes all 19 jobs on exact commit `eec27652c869eaf9965d7c3a06b873203f7eea7f`. Its Ubuntu log confirms the pinned source builds and the same HRTF and HOA sample counts and error metrics.

The independent MP3 VBRI and encoder expansion adds a pinned AndroidX Media asset whose hand-authored VBRI table uses the external convention that excludes its metadata frame from declared byte and frame counts. Memory and positional summaries agree on every scalar field, stride-one indexes validate all four TOC boundaries, and the stream decoder removes the metadata frame from audible PCM. The parser also accepts the defined Xing reserved and InfoTag flags used by the pinned Helix hmp3 5.2.4 encoder. That separately built encoder must produce a truthful variable-rate Xing table, `LAMEH5.24` identity, gapless fields, joint-stereo frames, and at least two bitrates before its complete PCM is compared with FFmpeg. Immutable source hashes, atomic acquisition, download, hash, build, and signal failures, all 24 decoder-probe failures, all 17 PCM-reference failures, and missing required Linux inputs are deterministic. The focused MP3 gate passes 129/129 tests with ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 412/412 steps and 7,380/7,386 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips, leaving a 5,249,764 KiB autonomous cache against the 67,108,864 KiB limit. GitHub Actions run `31438386591` passes all 19 jobs on exact commit `6f0e9509ee4ab771e9be4e86ce387c3f1e6d7cd1`. Its Ubuntu log confirms the deterministic runner, AndroidX VBRI decode and seek checks, and Helix metadata checks. The AndroidX FFmpeg comparison covers 269,568 samples with `0.000001085` normalized RMS error; the Helix comparison covers 105,840 samples with `0.000000692` normalized RMS error.

The independent Vorbis objective-quality gate passed focused macOS and native Ubuntu 24.04 AArch64 verification on 2026-08-06. A bounded raw-PCM probe compares external f32 or signed-16 little-endian decoder output directly with the source through `VorbisPcmQualityMeter` and rejects malformed byte counts, unequal sample counts, non-finite samples, and invalid retained measurements. The Linux gate requires FFmpeg and Xiph `oggdec`, decodes each deterministic q0, q5, and q10 project stream with both implementations, and enforces the existing normalized-RMS and signal-to-noise bounds on all six comparisons. FFmpeg measured normalized RMS errors of 0.556778313, 0.647156251, and 0.665769743 with signal-to-noise ratios of 5.086353785, 3.779816995, and 3.533518916 dB. Xiph measured 0.556318127, 0.647156306, and 0.665767112 with ratios of 5.093535778, 3.779816253, and 3.533553244 dB. The failure-injection runner requires every decoder and preset path and rejects missing metrics, malformed numbers, excessive error, and low signal-to-noise evidence. The focused gate passes 15/15 steps and 7/7 tests on both platforms, the combined Vorbis and cross-target gate passes 26/26 steps and 99/99 tests, and installed-package consumers pass 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 411/411 steps and 7,380/7,386 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips, leaving a 4,408,236 KiB autonomous cache against the 67,108,864 KiB limit. Public GitHub Actions run 31078308509 passed all 19 jobs on exact commit `e5704e8952d1acac4d67a2a77da14afc8be9b5d8`. Its Ubuntu job passed the six FFmpeg and Xiph q0, q5, and q10 objective-quality comparisons. Independent packet parity, concealment comparison, additional implementations, and audition remain open.

The independent Vorbis packet-loss gate pins Xiph libogg 1.3.5 and libvorbis 1.3.7 under their BSD-3-Clause-family licenses. The exact source archives have SHA-256 hashes `c4d91be36fc8e54deae7575241e03f4211eb102afb3fc0775fbbc1b740016705` and `b33cc4934322bcbf6efcbacf49e3ca01aadbea4114ec9589d1b1e9d20f72954b`. The external adapter uses libvorbis to encode deterministic streams, packetizes one complete packet per Ogg page, removes one complete audio packet outside the project, and performs overlap, granule accounting, and final trimming through libvorbis. Silence replacement advances the Xiph overlap state with a zero transform block. Signal replacement retains Xiph's preceding windowed transform block, center-crops or pads it to the missing size, applies the project's default linear decay from 1.0 to 0.5, reverses the target window, and advances the same Xiph overlap state. Five mono, stereo, single-link, and chained cases cover 256- and 2,048-sample losses, all mixed neighboring geometries, previous-block, following-header, and following-granule evidence, exact 144,000- or 288,000-frame terminal output, and Xiph's `OV_ENOTAUDIO` rejection of a damaged packet. The project matches 2,880,000 complete interleaved PCM values with peak error `5.305e-6` and normalized RMS error `1.214e-6`. The probe rejects silent, truncated, mismatched, non-finite, unexpectedly accepted, or transactionally modified references. Its source runner covers missing arguments, accepted installations, downloads, hashes, builds, TERM exit 143, exact failure statuses, and partial-directory cleanup. The dedicated build step fails explicitly without its native macOS or Linux target and pinned prefix. The focused native and supported cross-target Vorbis gate passes 14/14 steps and 93/93 tests. External interoperability passes 17/17 steps and 98/98 tests. Installed-package consumers pass 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 426/426 steps and 7,385/7,387 tests with the two documented platform-service skips. Public GitHub Actions run 31473947597 passes all 19 jobs on attempt 2 at commit `cd3bdf07f923c4d2532f6f03e36573368e32f3c7`; its required Ubuntu lane rebuilt both pinned Xiph archives, matched all 2,880,000 values with the same peak and normalized RMS errors, and passed the source preparation runner. The first Windows runner ended without a log blob, while its fresh retry and every downstream cross-compile, ABI, validator, pluginval, and LV2 distribution job passed. Additional implementation families and audition remain open.

The second independent Vorbis decoder gate pins the separately authored `stb_vorbis` 1.22 source at revision `2c980bb59875b0d32144a71867fbdebb2f77cd20`, SHA-256 `4c7cb2ff1f7011e9d67950446b7eb9ca044f2e464d76bfbb0b84dd2e23e65636`, under its public-domain or MIT terms. The adapter uses the memory API with integer conversion and stdio disabled, validates complete single-link Ogg framing before decode, and explicitly decodes compatible chains one logical link at a time because stb_vorbis does not concatenate streams. Eleven project-, FFmpeg-, and Xiph-authored cases cover mono, stereo, 8 through 48 kHz, low quality, 512- and 4,096-sample block families, mixed transitions, multi-page audio, and compatible chains. The project and stb_vorbis complete f32 output agrees with normalized RMS error no greater than `2.504e-6`; Xiph signed-16 output and stb_vorbis agree across 378,143 interleaved values with peak error no greater than `1.5378e-5` and normalized RMS error no greater than `7.8066e-5`. Checksum damage, truncated headers, truncated audio, a checksum-correct invalid audio packet, and incompatible chain geometry reject without replacing an existing output. Source acquisition tests cover missing arguments, exact installation reuse, identity mismatch, download failure, hash failure, TERM status 143, and partial cleanup. The exact native Ubuntu 24.04 AArch64 gate passes 12/12 steps and its unit test; focused Vorbis passes 24/24 steps and 98/98 tests with the Xiph reference, installed consumers pass 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 419/419 steps and 7,381/7,387 tests with six documented environment-dependent skips. Public GitHub Actions run `31534398241` passes all 19 jobs on exact commit `53b51d89304e530ed4e44fece8cec3dd7aac22c9`. Its Ubuntu lane verified the pinned source, passed the source runner and all five malformed-input cases, and compared all 378,143 values at peak error `1.5363e-5` and normalized RMS error `7.8066e-5`. A fixed-point decoder family and audition remain open.

The third Ubuntu CI repair candidate passed complete local and native Linux verification on 2026-08-05. Public run 31060323515 confirmed both adaptive gapless comparisons, the public HRTF fixtures, and the protected LAME comparison at all 30,870 values, then exposed `MissingExpectedMp3JointStereo` in the later FFmpeg-authored MPEG-2 stereo fixture. FFmpeg's libmp3lame `-joint_stereo 1` option permits joint-stereo decisions but does not force that header mode. Ubuntu LAME 3.100 selected ordinary stereo even for identical input channels, so the fixture asserted an encoder decision it had not actually requested. The MPEG-2 and MPEG-2.5 external joint-stereo fixtures now use native LAME's forced `-m j` mode and pin `--resample 22.05` or `--resample 11.025` to prevent bitrate-driven downsampling. The project probe still requires joint-stereo headers, and each complete decode still matches an independent FFmpeg f32 reference. A clean Ubuntu 24.04 AArch64 run with FFmpeg 6.1.1 and LAME 3.100 passes 19/19 focused steps and 133/133 tests. Its MPEG-2 comparison covers 18,432 interleaved values with normalized RMS error 0.000000445; its MPEG-2.5 comparison covers 10,368 values with normalized RMS error 0.000000322. Shell syntax, formatting, diff hygiene, the repository policy scanners and self-tests, the 22-decoder-probe and 15-reference-probe failure-injection runner, and the focused macOS gate also pass. The standalone installed-package suite passes 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 410/410 steps and 7,379/7,385 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips, leaving a 3,425,508 KiB autonomous cache against the 67,108,864 KiB limit.

Replacement run 31065544714 passed its macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows build-and-test jobs; repository hygiene; all six cross-compiles; both raw ABI jobs; Ubuntu validation and pluginval; and LV2 distribution. It then exposed one shared IR-loader defect in macOS and Windows validation and in macOS and Windows pluginval. The `PartitionedConvolver` processing preflight iterated its multi-megabyte `input_spectra` array by value on every process call, copying it onto host worker stacks. The macOS validator crash report identifies `validateProcessingSamples` at the stack guard. macOS pluginval reports `Bus error: 10` when it opens the editor during processing, while both Windows tools stop at the IR loader's first worker-thread or audio-processing call. Validation now walks all large convolver arrays by pointer, including prepared spectra, and a regression processes the production-size 131,072-frame convolver on a 512 KiB worker stack. The focused module passes 84/84 tests, the installed-package suite passes 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests, and native macOS Steinberg validation passes all 47 tests for both sample precisions, including both threaded-process tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 410/410 steps and 7,380/7,386 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips, leaving a 4,135,552 KiB autonomous cache against the 67,108,864 KiB limit. Replacement run 31071465341 passed all 19 jobs on exact commit `b82b6f8fdaa5e17767e39e8a039e878814e1b0eb`: all three platform build-and-test jobs, repository hygiene, all six cross-compiles, both raw ABI jobs, all three validators, all three pluginval jobs, and LV2 distribution. This closes the four IR-loader failures. The Ubuntu job matched the 1,513-measurement HUTUBS fixture across 128 frames and the 440-measurement SADIE II fixture across 256 frames. Its independent MP3 references passed for protected streams, adaptive gapless CBR and VBR, and forced joint-stereo MPEG-2 and MPEG-2.5. The MPEG-2 comparisons covered 18,432 samples at normalized RMS error 0.000000819, MPEG-2.5 covered 10,368 samples at 0.000001859, adaptive gapless CBR covered 6,912 samples at 0.002804251, and adaptive gapless VBR covered 6,912 samples at 0.002836473.

The second Ubuntu CI repair candidate passed complete local verification on 2026-08-05. Run 31038525428 confirmed that the earlier adaptive gapless MP3 and public HRTF repairs passed, then exposed a protected LAME stream whose project decode produced 36,864 interleaved values while FFmpeg produced 30,870. LAME 3.100 writes the protected MPEG-1 Info marker at byte 36, before the two-byte CRC displacement of ordinary main data. A broad MP3 refactor had accidentally replaced the dedicated Xing and Info offset with the physical main-data offset, so the parser looked at byte 38 and discarded LAME's delay 576 and padding 1,269. Xing and Info parsing and emission now share a distinct format offset while reservoir and audio payload handling retain the CRC-aware physical main-data offset. The protected metadata regression checks both offsets, validates the CRC, and requires parsed counts and gapless fields. With the corrected project metadata location, FFmpeg 6.1.1 omits the independent metadata frame but retains the four audio frames for the project's truthful non-LAME encoder identifier. The reference probe therefore accepts exactly three validated gapless layouts: audible-only PCM, PCM with the metadata frame omitted, or complete encoded PCM. It derives the comparison window for each layout from the validated gapless plan and format-defined metadata-frame length, and rejects every other length. A native Ubuntu 24.04 AArch64 reproduction passed both project adaptive gapless comparisons and the previously failing LAME protected comparison at 30,870 values with peak error 0.000000373 and normalized RMS error 0.000000369. That container later rejected an FFmpeg MPEG-2 fixture whose libmp3lame output ignored the requested joint-stereo mode. Replacement public x86-64 run 31060323515 reproduced that behavior after confirming the protected-frame repair. The local focused gate `scripts/zig_autonomous.sh build test-mp3 test-mp3-interop -Doptimize=ReleaseSafe --summary all` passes 19/19 steps and 133/133 tests. The staged installed-package suite passes 12/12 steps and 85/85 tests. `scripts/zig_autonomous.sh build test -Doptimize=ReleaseSafe --summary all` passes 410/410 steps and 7,379/7,385 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips. The aggregate completed with a 2,751,216 KiB autonomous cache, and the final focused rerun left 2,790,056 KiB, both against the 67,108,864 KiB limit. Run 31060323515 confirms the protected and adaptive gapless evidence; its later joint-stereo failure is addressed by the following candidate.

The Ubuntu CI repair candidate passed local verification on 2026-08-05. The failed run 31027508662 exposed two platform assumptions. FFmpeg 6.1 returned all five decoded frames for project-generated gapless MP3 fixtures whose truthful custom encoder identifier does not request FFmpeg-specific trimming, while the project comparison expected only the three audible frames. The PCM reference probe now has an explicit gapless comparison mode that accepts exactly either already-trimmed audible PCM or complete encoded PCM, derives the comparison window from the validated gapless plan, and rejects every other byte count. Only the adaptive gapless CBR and VBR comparisons opt into that mode. A direct unit test covers both accepted decoder behaviors plus malformed and unexpected lengths, and the deterministic runner verifies the argument policy and all 15 reference-probe failure paths. Ubuntu also installs NetCDF in a multiarch directory that Zig 0.16's Linux `std.DynLib` name search does not inspect. Linux SOFA loading now uses the native system dynamic loader, which honors the loader cache and resolves NetCDF's dependencies, while retaining the explicit runtime path and no NetCDF link-time dependency. Linux package and focused test roots declare the required libc boundary. `scripts/zig_autonomous.sh build test-mp3-interop test-hrtf -Doptimize=ReleaseSafe --summary all` passes 20/20 steps and 150/152 tests with the two expected unavailable platform-codec skips, including Linux x86-64 and AArch64 HRTF compilation. The staged installed-package suite passes 85/85 tests across 12/12 steps. `scripts/zig_autonomous.sh build test -Doptimize=ReleaseSafe --summary all` passes 410/410 steps and 7,379/7,385 tests with the six documented environment-dependent skips. The final autonomous cache measured 2,082,500 KiB against the 67,108,864 KiB limit. The public Ubuntu rerun and its downstream cross-compilation, ABI, validator, pluginval, and LV2 distribution jobs remain pending.

The current NetCDF runtime-discovery, public-fixture acquisition, and independent numerical-reference gate passed local verification on 2026-08-04. A Homebrew upgrade exposed that default macOS discovery named ABI 19 while the installed runtime used ABI 22 outside the system loader path. `HrtfSofaLoader.openDefault` now probes ABI names 19 through 22 plus stable Homebrew and MacPorts locations. `openRuntime` accepts an explicit product-selected path while retaining dynamic ownership and no link-time dependency. Empty and embedded-NUL runtime paths fail before allocation or native loading, allocator failure is transactional, and unavailable explicit libraries retain the existing runtime-unavailable result. The Viking and HUTUBS downloaders use unique adjacent partial files, convert HUP, INT, and TERM into conventional exits, and publish only after exact hash verification. Their deterministic runner covers accepted existing files, successful download, download failure, hash failure, TERM exit 143, status preservation, and complete partial-file cleanup for both sources. The exact pinned dataset hashes pass twice through the focused loader gate: once through the explicit ABI-22 path and once through zero-configuration discovery. A separately compiled C++ fixture links directly to NetCDF, reads source positions and responses independently, selects and weights its own neighbors, and performs its own direct transforms. One exact and three off-grid directions per dataset compare every sample from inverse-distance and spectral interpolation across both ears. All 6,144 samples match within the declared mixed absolute and relative tolerance. Short output storage and non-finite queries leave both output and result metadata unchanged. The focused HRTF gate passes 148/148 tests across 13/13 native, reference, acquisition-runner, and supported cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 tests across 12/12 steps and expose the explicit runtime API. The dataset-enabled complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 412/412 steps and 7,380/7,384 tests with four environment-dependent native-service and platform-codec skips. Comparison with an established acoustic renderer, product dataset policy, tracker integration, and headphone audition remain open.

The independent HOA matrix and render gate passed local verification on 2026-08-04. A separately compiled C++ fixture evaluates the real spherical-harmonic basis from an explicit Legendre-polynomial derivative, builds the full-row-rank pseudoinverse through a pivoted Gram-system inversion, and applies its own max-rE order weights. It shares no basis, SVD, matrix, or decoder implementation with the framework. The gate compares 336 basis values across SN3D, N3D, FuMa, all components through order three, poles, axes, and off-axis directions. A 32-speaker golden-angle layout and a regular 8-by-4 full-sphere grid, each with an LFE row, cover basic and max-rE generation for all three normalizations, canonical and deliberately permuted component orderings, and mixed linear and decibel component gains. All 12,672 coefficients and 203,544 rendered samples match within declared relative tolerances. Invalid basis order and short coefficient storage leave caller output unchanged. The focused HOA gate filters its imported dependency graph to the 20 HOA-specific tests, including the ADM XML HOA metadata test, while the public-core and aggregate gates retain all ADM XML tests. It passes 20/20 tests across 11/11 native, reference, and supported cross-target steps. A cold filtered run completes in 11.79 seconds, with 220 milliseconds spent running tests, instead of the previous two-minute 205-test dependency sweep. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 tests across 12/12 steps. The complete ReleaseSafe graph containing the expanded oracle passes 408/408 steps and 7,378/7,384 tests with six environment-dependent skips. The earlier dataset-enabled graph passes 412/412 steps and 7,380/7,384 tests with four environment-dependent skips. Comparison with established HOA renderers, full-system measurement, production condition and gain policy, and physical loudspeaker audition remain open.

The Vorbis quality-report parser hardening gate passed focused local verification on 2026-08-04. The q0, q5, and q10 calibration now requires exactly one decimal or scientific-notation value for each normalized-error and signal-to-noise field. Missing, duplicated, malformed, NaN, explicit infinity, and numerically overflowing reports fail before the calibration comparison. The failure-injection runner covers every rejection plus excessive error, low signal-to-noise ratio, and identical encoded fixtures. Shell syntax, diff hygiene, and the retired-reference checker pass. The public quality scale remains a psychoacoustic request without a monotonic raw-error or bitrate promise. Independent external quality comparison and audition remain open.

The production termination-path policy hardening gate passed local verification on 2026-08-04. The scanner now checks comment- and string-free source instead of raw lines, covers `std.debug.panic` and `@trap` in addition to runtime assertions, `@panic`, and `unreachable`, and continues to exclude test bodies. Its fixture rejects every production termination form, accepts the same tokens in comments and strings, and proves that scanning resumes after a test block. The live production tree, raw callback policy and self-test, shell syntax, diff hygiene, and the retired-reference checker pass. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this repository policy.

The fixed and dynamic SVD sweep-budget construction binding passed complete verification on 2026-08-04. Both decomposition owners now retain the configured maximum sweep count. Validation rejects zero completed or maximum counts, a completed count beyond the configured budget, and a nonconverged result that did not exhaust its exact budget. Wide decompositions transfer the same construction value, and dynamic repeatable teardown clears it. Direct regressions mutate converged and nonconverged fixed and dynamic results, require rejection before solving or condition reporting, restore the retained budget, and prove successful recovery. The installed-package consumer contains public fixed and dynamic converged mutation and recovery coverage. The focused matrix gate passes 37/37 tests across all 6/6 native and supported cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 404/404 steps and 7,378/7,384 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-state contract.

The installed-package temporary-artifact containment gate passed its deterministic runners and complete integration rerun on 2026-08-04. Failed installed-package builds now remove their staged package and private compiler cache by default. Setting `ZIG_VST3_KEEP_FAILED_INSTALL_PACKAGE=1` preserves exactly one requested diagnostic tree, removes its active marker, moves it from the live-only namespace into the completed namespace, and prints its path. Invalid environment settings and command arguments fail before creating a temporary directory. A fake compiler runner proves success cleanup, ordinary failure cleanup, explicit preservation, invalid-setting rejection, namespace transfer, exact failure propagation, and conventional TERM exit without invoking a compiler. The interruption case requests failure preservation, signals the actual runner process, requires exit 143, and proves that no active or completed staging tree remains. Its invalid-setting case runs in a subshell so POSIX shells cannot retain the deliberately invalid assignment and contaminate the following argument-validation case. The runner passes repeatedly under macOS `/bin/sh` and Bash. Every live staging tree uses a name that the cleaner cannot match and also carries an active marker. `scripts/clean_installed_package_artifacts.sh inspect` enumerates completed trees without changing them. Its apply mode preflights every matching path before deletion, refuses symbolic links and non-directories, removes only completed trees, and retains marked completed names plus every live-namespace tree. A disposable runner covers inspection, completed cleanup, marked retention, live-namespace isolation, later completed cleanup, empty state, invalid modes, and symlink refusal. The 33 historical completed trees were removed after explicit approval. The real staged suite passes 85/85 tests across 12/12 steps both independently and inside the aggregate, and inspection confirms that neither run left a completed staging tree.

The LU and QR scale-derived tolerance construction binding passed complete verification on 2026-08-04. Fixed and dynamic LU plus fixed and dynamic QR now retain the finite positive maximum magnitude of their source matrix. Validation recomputes the exact pivot or rank tolerance from that construction scale and the retained dimensions before examining pivots or reflectors. Fixed LU now uses the same relative threshold policy as the dynamic owner instead of comparing every pivot against an absolute machine epsilon. Fixed LU and fixed QR solve well-conditioned f64 systems at `1e-200` scale. One matched dynamic fixture proves LU, QR, and relative-threshold SVD least-squares solving at the same scale. The installed-package consumer covers fixed f32 LU at `1e-20` plus dynamic f64 LU, QR, and SVD at `1e-200`. Mutating either the scale or tolerance can no longer change the admitted numerical boundary while leaving the decomposition valid. Dynamic teardown clears both scale and tolerance. Direct regressions cover independent scale and tolerance mutation, rejection before determinant or least-squares operations, unchanged caller destinations and workspaces, restoration, and successful recovery. The installed-package consumer contains public fixed LU, fixed QR, dynamic LU, and dynamic QR scale-mutation recovery coverage. The focused matrix gate passes 37/37 tests across all 6/6 native and supported cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 404/404 steps and 7,378/7,384 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this construction contract.

The local cache-budget preflight and postflight passed their deterministic runners and cold integration rebuild on 2026-08-04. `scripts/check_zig_cache_budget.sh` measures the ordinary repository cache, or the autonomous cache with `--autonomous`, against a 64 GiB default. `ZIG_VST3_CACHE_LIMIT_KIB` accepts a bounded positive integer override. Missing caches pass without mutation. Oversized caches fail with the measured and configured sizes plus the exact inspection and guarded cleanup commands. The checker refuses symbolic links, non-directory cache paths, invalid limits, invalid arguments, and repositories without the build marker. `scripts/zig_autonomous.sh` checks before and after invoking the selected Zig executable with repository-local autonomous global and local caches. It forwards every compiler argument unchanged, never starts Zig after preflight rejection, reports growth beyond the budget after a run, and preserves the compiler status when both the compiler and postflight fail. Disposable runners cover both cache selections, absent, within-budget, and over-budget states, every malformed limit class, unsafe path shapes, the missing marker, exact cache environment, exact argument forwarding, missing arguments, compiler suppression after rejection, postflight growth, compiler failure, and combined failure. The autonomous cache is ignored by Git. Both runners pass under macOS `/bin/sh` and Bash. The 95,674,476 KiB cache was removed after explicit approval. The complete cold verification chain rebuilt a 1,026,036 KiB cache, remains below the 67,108,864 KiB limit, and leaves 182 GiB available.

The transactional bundle-publication gate passed its deterministic runners on 2026-08-04. Linux, Windows, macOS VST3, macOS Audio Unit, and LV2 publishers convert HUP, INT, and TERM into conventional nonzero exits. Staging cleanup leaves the previously published bundle intact before publication. Replacement first moves an existing bundle to an adjacent backup, publishes the completed staging tree, and removes the backup only after success. Cleanup restores the backup when the final move fails. Fake copy or metadata tools deliver TERM during staging, and fake move tools reject the final publication step. Every invocation rejects the interrupted or failed publication, preserves the old marker, and leaves no staging or backup directory. Successful replacement of every bundle format is also covered. Shell syntax, ShellCheck, and diff hygiene pass. The focused LV2 gate passes 56/56 steps and 569/569 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 404/404 steps and 7,378/7,384 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host or manual confirmation is required for this filesystem contract.

The ADM construction-bound count gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-04. Static, fixed-delay, and variable Matrix coefficient mixers now require their live input and term counts to match the resolved construction-time plan. The HOA matrix decoder binds both live dimensions, the radial filter bank binds its live input count, both direct-speaker routers bind their live output count, Objects gain plans bind their output layout, Objects timelines bind both the output layout and declared block sequence, and Binaural timelines bind both per-ear block sequences. Forged counts within unused capacity can no longer admit undeclared channels, drop declared terms, motion segments, or ear-gain segments, reinterpret zero-initialized capacity as a render contribution, or enlarge a resolved speaker route. Stateful mixer and radial resets preserve malformed count state, cursors, absolute positions, and histories so the caller can diagnose or repair the state. Direct regressions cover all ten processors, rejected output preservation, and reset preservation. Installed-package consumers exercise the same public boundaries. The focused HOA gate passes 203/203 tests across 6/6 native and supported cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 across 12/12 steps. Repository policies, codec interoperability runners, visual regression execution, platform ABI gates, and the retired-reference checker pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,376/7,382 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for these retained-state contracts.

The HRTF dataset, motion-plan, streaming publication, and room-asset construction-bound gates passed complete verification on 2026-08-04. Databases bind live measurement, response-frame, and rendered-frame counts to the imported dataset. Motion renderers bind their live sample rate, filter length, point count, and crossfade length to the latest successful preparation. Streaming renderers bind their start and crossfade configuration, while every published slot binds its frame count and sample rate to the database used for preparation. Producer preflight validates the latest immutable submission. Consumer preflight validates the complete pending slot sequence without crossing the SPSC ownership boundary. First-order, second-order, and bounded image-source room plans bind their live path counts to the constructed route set. Room-surface impulse responses bind all six live frame counts and their live sample rate to the imported material set. Reducing a count within fixed capacity can no longer discard measurements, shorten responses, truncate scheduled motion, omit room routes, or shorten material responses while remaining valid. Direct and installed-package regressions cover rejection before interpolation, composition, or rendering and exact preservation of queue publication, caller output, renderer history, and sample position. The concurrent filter-transfer regression passes ten consecutive optimized stress runs. The focused HRTF gate passes 146/148 tests across 6/6 native and supported cross-target steps, with only two optional public datasets skipped. Installed-package consumers pass 85/85 across 12/12 steps. Repository policies, codec interoperability runners, visual regression execution, platform ABI gates, LV2 distribution and host harnesses, and the retired-reference checker pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,376/7,382 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, tracker, headphone, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for these construction contracts.

The SVD basis, MP3 masking-history, and Vorbis quality-energy retained-state gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-04. Fixed and dynamic SVD decompositions now retain their convergence tolerance and verify pairwise orthogonality for every rotation-derived basis column plus each normalized column inside the numerical rank. Rank-deficient discarded directions remain valid, while duplicated result-bearing columns fail before solve, reconstruction, condition, or pseudoinverse operations. MP3 psychoacoustic timelines reject a right-channel history without the left-channel history that frame-order publication necessarily creates first. Vorbis quality meters require exact matches to retain equal reference, candidate, and cross energy, and require either silent side to retain zero cross energy plus the exact opposite-side error energy. Direct and installed-package regressions cover rejection, unchanged hostile state, rank-deficient polynomial fitting, and successful recovery. The dynamic SVD retained-basis normalization gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Validation now recomputes every left and right basis-column magnitude with scaled accumulation. The standalone installed-package runner now defaults to ReleaseSafe. Its previous implicit Debug mode spent four to five minutes in the 32-test DSP consumer. The low-level MP3 and Vorbis encoder overlap audit passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. The transactional positional seek-index gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. `buildMp3FileSeekIndexTransactional` and `buildVorbisFileSeekIndexTransactional` stage their complete construction scan in caller-owned point scratch before replacing the destination. File truncation, malformed framing, insufficient destination or scratch, storage overlap, growth beyond staged capacity, or a point-count change between sizing and construction cannot expose a partial index. Direct tests compare successful transactional output with the existing memory and file indexes and preserve sentinel destinations across truncation, both capacity failures, and alias rejection. Both methods are exported through the installed package and exercised by its public consumer. The focused MP3 suite passes 129/129 tests, the Vorbis suite passes 90/90, and both native and cross-target build matrices pass. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies and codec interoperability pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,351/7,357 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this publication contract.

The transactional MP3 positional-iteration gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. `Mp3FileReader.nextTransactional` stages one complete frame in disjoint caller scratch and publishes state plus destination bytes only after successful framing and metadata parsing. Overlap, short destination, short scratch, malformed input, and a file truncated after reader initialization leave both reader and destination unchanged. VBRI table slices are rebound to destination storage before publication, so returned metadata never borrows scratch. The exhaustive positional mutation matrix now applies this API to every byte of the second frame under three masks and to every truncation boundary. Direct tests also cover success, retry after failure, end of stream, and borrowed-table provenance. The focused MP3 matrix passes 128/128 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps and exercise the public method. Repository policies and codec interoperability pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,350/7,356 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this publication contract.

The positional codec mutation gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. The MP3 file reader mutates every byte of a retained second frame under three masks and tests every truncation boundary while preserving complete reader state after rejection. The FLAC file reader applies the same byte mutations and strict prefixes while transactional decoding preserves caller output after initialization or decode failure. The uncompressed positional reader covers WAV, AIFF, AIFC, RF64, BW64, and Wave64 under the same mutation masks and every strict prefix. Accepted variants remain valid and bounded, failed transactional reads preserve output, and all truncated uncompressed containers are rejected during initialization. Focused ReleaseSafe tests pass 126/126 for MP3, 39/39 for FLAC, and 195/195 for the shared uncompressed reader. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies and codec interoperability pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,348/7,354 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this malformed-input hardening.

The shared file-writer sync-failure gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. `FileWriterOperations` now routes final synchronization through the same injectable backend as positional writes, length changes, rollback, and recovery. WAV, AIFF, RF64, Wave64, incremental FLAC, Ogg, and every operation-backed MP3 path use that callback. Direct regressions inject final sync failures into all four PCM container writers, FLAC, Ogg, and both exact-gapless adaptive MP3 file writers. Each owner remains valid or explicitly recoverable and completes the same finalization after the injected fault is cleared. Focused ReleaseSafe tests pass 184/184 for the shared PCM fault contract, 38/38 for FLAC, 90/90 for Ogg, and 121/121 across the six-step MP3 matrix. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps. Formatting, diff hygiene, callback, termination-path, retired-reference, codec interoperability, GUI, accessibility, sanitizer, ABI, bundle, and cross-target gates pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph remains at 400/400 steps and 7,341/7,347 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this storage-failure work.

The strict positional MP3 recovery and seek gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Memory and file resynchronization now parse each complete candidate frame before committing a recovered offset. Both paths reject malformed Xing or VBRI metadata, invalid protected CRCs, and malformed side information instead of accepting a header-shaped false positive. Positional seek applies the same checks to the complete target frame before changing its byte, frame, or sample position. Deterministic regressions place each malformed frame class between valid audio frames, prove exact and insufficient recovery limits, preserve both readers on rejection, and accept the following valid frame. The focused MP3 matrix passes 123/123 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies and interoperability pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,343/7,349 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this parser hardening.

The protected MP3 Xing and Info placement gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Metadata encoding and parsing now use the actual main-data boundary, including the two-byte CRC field before side information. Protected metadata no longer overwrites the final side-information bytes or places its marker two bytes before the standard location. Direct coverage checks the exact marker offset, absence at the former offset, parsed counts, and repaired protected CRC. Existing protected exact-gapless CBR and VBR paths remain green. The focused MP3 matrix passes 123/123 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, the generated interoperability gate passes, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,343/7,349 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Independent decoding remains unavailable in this macOS environment, so the external Linux decoder comparison remains open. No host, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the byte-layout correction.

The strict free-format MP3 inference gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Memory and positional-file inference now validate the complete first, candidate, and following frames before accepting an inferred base length. Validation includes metadata, side information, and protected CRCs. Two aligned header patterns inside the first frame no longer establish a false 100-byte base when their implied side information is malformed. Protected padded and unpadded 500-byte streams retain exact framing, seek, CRC, and bounded resynchronization behavior after the stricter inference. The focused MP3 matrix passes 125/125 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies and interoperability pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,345/7,351 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this framing hardening.

The transactional VBRI stream-finalization gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. `finalizeMp3VbriStreamMetadata` scans a completed stream into caller-owned frame-offset storage, builds the scaled VBRI table in caller storage, requires an existing reserved first audio frame, stages only that frame, and leaves encoded bytes unchanged on every failure. The interoperability fixture now uses this public path instead of duplicating the complete stream through an allocator. Direct tests cover successful lookup, a missing reservation, protected-frame rejection, and unchanged failure output. The focused MP3 matrix passes 118/118 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, and the focused interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,338/7,344 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Independent VBRI authorship and audition remain external work.

The exact-gapless adaptive stream gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Separate CBR and VBR coordinators reserve an independent Info or Xing frame without granting reservoir credit, advance the padding and analysis cadence, stream caller PCM through the existing bounded publisher, and flush the 1,057-sample analysis delay during finalization. Finish uses disjoint caller rollback and output staging, restores the complete pending window after any error, and does not touch the destination until every flush and retained frame succeeds. CBR derives exact final metadata directly. VBR derives quality and a 100-entry seek table from the completed streamed bytes. Both outputs match their exact-gapless batch counterparts byte for byte and expose exact audible sample counts. Direct tests cover protected CRCs, borrowing, short output after staged VBR work, overlap, repeated finish, and hostile independent-frame state. The focused MP3 matrix passes 119/119 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,339/7,345 tests with six environment-dependent skips.

The recoverable exact-gapless adaptive file gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. CBR and VBR positional writers publish a reserved placeholder before accepting PCM, retain the last committed boundary, stage delayed-tail output, and replace metadata only after the complete stream is known. A failed tail write, first-frame rewrite, or sync rolls encoder state back and leaves the writer recoverable. Recovery truncates uncommitted tail bytes and restores the placeholder before allowing a retry. Public sizing functions report finish staging and the exact final VBR offset count. VBR uses caller-owned frame offsets to build its final 100-entry Xing table without retaining the complete encoded stream. A late offset-capacity failure rejects the current PCM frame before file mutation, and `replaceFrameOffsetStorage` copies the committed index into a larger disjoint slice before retry. Retained offsets must start at zero, increase strictly, remain inside the committed byte range, and delimit exact frame lengths allowed by the configured bitrate range. Direct fault injection covers a partial initial-placeholder write and a metadata-rewrite failure after tail publication, recovers both formats, retries, and proves both files byte-for-byte identical to their exact batch counterparts. Direct validation also rejects missing or undersized VBR frame-index storage, inconsistent retained counts, and malformed retained offsets, including a one-byte span corruption. The focused MP3 matrix passes 120/120 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,340/7,346 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this file-state work. The next code-only MP3 work is broader independent codec interoperability and malformed-stream hardening.

The adaptive gapless MP3 interoperability fixture gate passed its local project and runner verification on 2026-08-03. The deterministic generator now emits protected MPEG-1 joint-stereo CBR and VBR streams through the exact-gapless adaptive reservoir composers. The project probe requires complete Info or Xing counts, nonzero delay and padding fields, and a nonempty trimmed range smaller than the encoded extent. The runner covers both new decoder calls and both independent PCM-reference calls, then injects failures at all 19 decoder and 12 reference-probe positions. The focused interoperability build passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests. AudioToolbox cannot decode these files in the current environment, so the real FFmpeg trimmed-PCM comparisons remain open for Linux execution. No host, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the deterministic fixture and failure-containment work.

The MP3 multi-frame reservoir transport gate passed on 2026-08-03. `mp3ReservoirRepackRequirements` measures a complete untagged stream of independently encoded compatible frames, and `repackMp3MainDataReservoir` stages their logical payloads across the full caller-bounded 511-byte MPEG-1 or 255-byte MPEG-2 and MPEG-2.5 history window. The transformation uses disjoint caller scratch, updates every main-data backpointer, repairs protected-frame CRCs, rejects metadata, free-format, format-changing, and already packed streams, and commits the destination only after complete success. A zero history limit preserves the original independent encoding. Direct coverage proves exact decoded PCM equivalence, a 511-byte backpointer spanning more than one preceding frame, CRC validity, scratch-suffix preservation, and unchanged rejection for invalid limits, insufficient storage, and overlap. The focused MP3 suite passes 109/109 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps. The interoperability runner accepts all four generated streams with its expected unavailable AudioToolbox skip. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,329/7,335 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Quantizer-aware adaptive reservoir budgeting, independent decoder exchange where external tools are unavailable, and audition remain open. No host, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the transport primitive.

The MP3 reservoir quantizer-budget foundation passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. `mp3ReservoirQuantizerBudget` validates caller-reported history against the format's 511-byte or 255-byte limit and reports physical, history, and total logical bits. The logical total is capped by the active granule and channel side-information fields. `Mp3EncoderQuantizer.processWithReservoirMasking` applies that budget to the existing energy-weighted channel allocation, while ordinary quantization delegates with zero history and retains its prior behavior. Direct coverage exercises both format limits, side-information saturation, invalid limits, free-format rejection, and a difficult 64 kb/s frame whose expanded history budget produces a strictly lower noise-to-mask ratio than its physical-only budget. The focused MP3 gate passes 110/110 tests and all three ReleaseSafe cross-target builds. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps. Repository policies and the deterministic interoperability runner pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,330/7,336 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Automatic credit tracking and serialization of expanded quantized payloads through the multi-frame transport remain open. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the pure budget and quantizer boundary.

The adaptive multi-frame MP3 encoder, incremental CBR and VBR publishers, recoverable incremental files, atomic batch files, basic metadata, and exact gapless metadata gate passed complete verification on 2026-08-03. Quantized-frame parts separate side information from as many as 2,048 bytes of logical main data, and `packMp3MainDataReservoir` places a complete sequence through disjoint caller scratch before committing its destination. `Mp3ReservoirCreditTracker` derives each frame's reachable history from actual capacity and byte-rounded logical use, caps retained credit to caller policy and format limits, and rejects malformed state, format changes, and overspending without mutation. `encodeMp3PcmReservoirBatch` composes that state with PCM analysis and quantization. `encodeMp3VbrPcmReservoirBatch` evaluates every bitrate candidate against the same reachable credit and returns exact bitrate, quality, payload, borrowing, backpointer, and retained-credit evidence. The adaptive stream encoders retain caller-owned frames only while their main-data regions remain reachable by future backpointers. They publish every newly immutable prefix during append and release the bounded tail during finish. Direct comparison proves byte-for-byte equivalence with complete CBR and VBR batch packing, while short output and alias failures preserve encoder state and pending bytes. The incremental file encoders snapshot the unpublished window in disjoint caller storage before each operation. An injected partial write restores the stream state and pending bytes; recovery truncates to the last committed boundary before the same PCM frame is retried. The four batch file functions finish encoding before file mutation, replace bytes at a checked audio offset, truncate stale trailing data, and synchronize the exact result. Their Info and Xing variants prepend truthful total-frame and byte counts; Xing also carries optional quality and a generated 100-entry seek table. Those compatibility variants leave delay and padding unspecified because they do not flush the analysis delay. The exact-gapless variants reserve an independent metadata frame outside reservoir credit, advance analysis through it, flush the 1,057-sample delay, and derive final trims from encoded extent and input samples. Existing single-frame and ordinary VBR paths delegate with zero history and retain their prior behavior. Direct regressions also require a logical payload larger than its own frame body, observe a 511-byte cross-frame backpointer, decode finite nonsilent PCM, validate every CRC, contain hostile credit, and preserve destinations after invalid limits, insufficient logical storage, and aliasing. The focused MP3 gate passes 117/117 tests across 6/6 native and cross-target steps. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps and exercise both adaptive stream and file publishers. Repository policies and the MP3 interoperability runner pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,337/7,343 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Broader independent encoder and decoder exchange and audition remain open. No host, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the adaptive encoders or their recoverable file paths.

The dynamic decomposition retained-shape gate passed on 2026-08-03. LU, QR, and SVD validation now evaluates each checked storage product before the surrounding boolean condition. An overflowing forged dimension can no longer turn a failed check into a true result through `catch` expression precedence or reach retained-factor indexing. Matrix and decomposition teardown remains safe to repeat, and later operations reject the closed value. Direct and installed-package regressions cover maximum retained dimensions, unchanged ownership, repeated teardown, and post-teardown rejection for the matrix and all three decomposition types. The focused matrix gate passes 35/35 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps. Repository policy checks pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,325/7,331 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required.

The HRTF motion-timeline containment gate passed on 2026-08-03. Preparation now rejects any point whose fixed crossfade cannot finish before the end of the `u64` sample timeline. Quiescent validation and realtime preflight also require the live sample cursor to have reached its current point and prevent the prior point from remaining active after its transition window. Early, overdue, and unfinishable schedules return silence or a checked error without changing filter history, sample position, or the previously prepared schedule. Direct and installed-package regressions cover all three cases. The focused HRTF gate passes 142/144 tests with only two optional public datasets skipped, including ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps. Repository policies pass. The complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,325/7,331 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, device, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the timeline invariant.

The MP3 reservoir borrowed-counter containment gate passed on 2026-08-03. Constant-rate, VBR, and reservoir-backed VBR stream validation now bounds cumulative borrowed main-data bytes by the number of possible predecessor frames and the format's 511-byte MPEG-1 or 255-byte MPEG-2 and MPEG-2.5 history limit. A restored or corrupted total that no encoded frame sequence could produce is rejected before append, finish, summary, or file composition. Direct and installed-package regressions cover all three public owners and preserve their retained state on rejection. Exact boundary tests cover each format limit, the zero-frame case, and the saturated upper range. The focused MP3 gate passes 108/108 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, the deterministic interoperability runner and repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,326/7,332 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the retained-counter invariant.

The MP3 pending-reservoir accounting gate passed on 2026-08-03. Fixed-rate, VBR, and VBR stream validation now requires the pending frame's encoded main-data backpointer to fit within the cumulative borrowed-byte total. After subtracting that backpointer, the remainder must be reachable by the preceding frame count and version-specific history limit. Direct and installed-package regressions cover all three public owners, exact boundary arithmetic, and unchanged rejected state. The focused MP3 suite passes 108/108 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. The independent interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests with the expected unavailable AudioToolbox decoder skip. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-accounting invariant.

The expanded generated MP3 interoperability gate passed on 2026-08-03. The deterministic fixture now adds MPEG-2 mono and MPEG-2 intensity-stereo streams to the protected MPEG-1 joint-stereo and VBRI cases. Memory and positional project decoders validate all four generated formats. The runner self-test exercises both new independent-reference paths and fails on every individual decoder or PCM-reference probe. The focused interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests. Repository policies pass, installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. FFmpeg is unavailable in the current environment, so real independent PCM comparison of the two new generated streams remains open. No host, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the fixture expansion itself.

The Vorbis frame-timeline containment gate passed on 2026-08-03. Frame-planner validation now relates every retained packet count to the minimum and maximum PCM-center progress possible under the format's legal block-size range. Zero-center exhausted cursors and other impossible restored or corrupted positions are rejected before planning. Direct boundary regressions cover minimum and maximum one-packet centers, impossible adjacent values, a representable overflow edge, maximum packet count, unchanged state on rejection, and the installed public package. The focused Vorbis gate passes 90/90 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, the deterministic interoperability and quality runners pass, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,326/7,332 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for the retained-timeline invariant.

The HOA radial retained-policy gate passed on 2026-08-03. Radial-bank validation now rejects a screen-referenced bank whose retained policy has changed to reject that rendering mode. It also requires the exact first- and second-order cascade length implied by each HOA order and the optional gain-limiting shelf. Corrupted policy or shortened retained cascades fail before processing and preserve filter history and output. Direct regressions cover both cases, exact state preservation, and the public installed-package boundary. The focused HOA gate passes 200/200 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,326/7,332 tests with six environment-dependent skips. External renderer comparison, physical loudspeaker measurement, and audition remain separate manual evidence.

The streaming HRTF sample-timeline gate passed on 2026-08-03. Producer preflight now requires every scheduled filter to leave a representable sample position after its complete crossfade. Quiescent validation also requires every queued point to follow the renderer start, remain at least one crossfade after its predecessor, and own any retained fade that claims to be adopting it. Consumer validation applies the timeline bound when a late filter becomes active and rejects retained startup or replacement fades once their completion position has been reached. Unfinishable scheduled, dense, late, early-origin, and overdue transitions fail before slot publication, history mutation, cursor advancement, or audio output. Direct and installed-package regressions cover the public schedule, queue, and consumer boundaries. The focused HRTF gate passes 144/146 tests with only two optional public datasets skipped, including ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. Live tracker transport and headphone audition remain separate product and manual evidence.

The FLAC file-writer committed-state gate passed on 2026-08-03. `FlacFileWriter.valid` now means the owner can accept another PCM append and therefore excludes a finalizing owner, even when that owner remains recoverable. Recovery validation relates committed sample and frame counts, audio byte extent, and minimum and maximum encoded frame sizes to one reachable stream state before append, finalization, or recovery can perform file I/O. Direct regressions cover inconsistent zero-frame state, a noncanonical initial frame-size bound, finalizing append rejection, exact state preservation, and the existing injected size-overflow path. The focused FLAC suite passes 38/38 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-state invariant.

The Ogg writer page-state reachability gate passed on 2026-08-03. The memory and file writers now share an allocation-free retained-state check that binds the began and ended lifecycle, committed byte extent, and 32-bit page sequence to at least one reachable page count. It derives the possible page-count interval from Ogg's exact minimum and maximum encoded page sizes and accepts sequence rollover when the byte extent can actually contain it. Impossible initial sequence state, ended-before-began state, and page counts too large for their byte extent fail before destination or file mutation. Direct regressions preserve the complete owner on rejection, and the installed public package covers the memory-writer boundary. The focused Ogg and Vorbis suite passes 90/90 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-state invariant.

The Vorbis encoder granule-lag gate passed on 2026-08-03. `VorbisPcmPacketSequence.valid` now binds every published audio granule to the retained frame center using the exact advance envelope implied by legal 64-to-8,192-sample blocks. Active streams require a 32-to-4,096-sample lag. A finalized stream permits at most 8,192 samples so legal terminal trimming can retain the preceding published boundary. A forged granule equal to the live center is rejected before terminal planning, with the complete sequence preserved. Direct and installed-package regressions cover that public boundary. The focused Ogg and Vorbis suite passes 90/90 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-state invariant.

The Vorbis decoder retained-timeline gate passed on 2026-08-03. Granule validation now rejects a known position offset when its current signed PCM endpoint overflows or remains negative. Stream validation relates every packet after priming to the 32-to-4,096-sample progress possible under legal block transitions. Chained validation requires its published output count to equal the nested granule output while active and not exceed it after terminal trimming. Forged endpoint, packet-count, decoded-sample, and chained-output states fail before decode, concealment, or logical-stream mutation. Direct and installed-package regressions cover the public owners and preserve rejected state. The focused Ogg and Vorbis suite passes 90/90 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for these retained-state invariants.

The MP3 reader byte-progress gate passed on 2026-08-03. Memory-stream and positional-reader validation now require the retained byte cursor to have advanced far enough to contain every completed frame at the smallest compatible Layer III frame size. This remains valid after bounded resynchronization because skipped bytes only increase cursor progress. Positional seeking applies the same lower bound before header I/O, so a target cannot attach an oversized frame index to an early valid header. Direct and installed-package regressions cover rewound memory state, a completed positional frame at the initial offset, impossible seek progress, unchanged owners, and unchanged caller frame storage. The focused MP3 suite passes 108/108 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. The generated interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests with the expected unavailable AudioToolbox decoder skip. Installed-package consumers pass 82/82 tests across 12/12 steps, repository policies pass, and the complete ReleaseSafe graph passes 400/400 steps and 7,328/7,334 tests with six environment-dependent skips. No host, decoder exchange, audible, visual, or manual confirmation is required for this retained-state invariant.

The MP3 PCM decoder frame-cadence gate passed on 2026-08-03. Initialization now requires a nonempty summary whose encoded sample count equals its frame count times the exact frame length implied by its sample rate. Retained decoding state must remain on the same 1,152-sample MPEG-1 or 576-sample lower-rate frame cadence before decode or finish. Direct and installed-package regressions cover malformed summaries, fractional-frame cursors, state preservation, and the public package boundary. The focused MP3 suite passes 108/108 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. The generated interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 3/3 tests with the expected unavailable AudioToolbox decoder skip. The Ogg reader lifecycle-containment gate passed on 2026-08-03. Memory and positional page readers now bind the absent-serial state to the canonical initial lifecycle or the explicit chained-page reload checkpoint, reject continuation or EOS before any nonchained logical stream begins, and prevent nonchained readers from claiming a later logical-stream index. Direct and installed-package regressions cover malformed lifecycle combinations, state preservation, and the public package boundary. The focused Ogg and Vorbis suite passes 90/90 tests and ReleaseSafe compilation for Linux AArch64, Linux x86-64, and Windows x86-64 GNU. The independent interoperability gate passes 11/11 steps and 95/95 tests with the expected unavailable AudioToolbox decoder skip.
